Model before rewriting:

letting b be 3
letting k be 3
letting l be 20
letting r be 60
letting v be 3
find bibd: matrix indexed by [int(1..v), int(1..b)] of int(0..1)
find x: int(-5..5)
find y: int(-5..5)

such that

bibd[bibd[x, y], bibd[x, y]] = 1

--

bibd
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
bibd
new variables:
  find bibd#components_1: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_2: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_3: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_4: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_5: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_6: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_7: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_8: int(0..1)
  find bibd#components_9: int(0..1)

--

bibd
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
bibd

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#int_rank_1: bool
  find x#int_rank_2: bool
  find x#int_rank_3: bool
  find x#int_rank_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#int_rank_4),and([!(x#int_rank_3),or([!(x#int_rank_2),and([!(x#int_rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
